# OpenIDEA parity matrix

OpenIDEA is the product specification, Mayoi/MyGo is the frontend base and the
OpenIDEA Rust core is the engine. This document enumerates everything the pinned
OpenIDEA source registers and maps each item to GoIDEA. It is generated from
`8f338999ec4f9dfe89173c07fd4069c8f82ff0d3`; `scripts/openidea-parity.py --check` fails when it is stale.

## How to read the status

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `integrated` | GoIDEA has the feature and a gate check proves it. |
| `partial` | Part of the feature exists in GoIDEA, with evidence. |
| `engine-only` | The engine behind it is reachable over stdio, but no GoIDEA UI proof exists. |
| `blocked` | It needs an OpenIDEA change (an `ops` module, or a Host/crate change) first. |
| `missing` | No GoIDEA implementation is recorded. |

`missing` is the default and is not a claim that no code exists anywhere: it means
no evidence has been recorded. A registered action with an otherwise undeclared
status appears as `partial`; other raised statuses come from
`scripts/openidea-parity-status.json`, where evidence names a regression check id,
a doc or a source path. Nothing is hand-picked out of the inventory: the item
lists below are generated from the source, and a new action, tool window, page or
command appears automatically.

are not product features; the contributions they host are listed below.

| `eclipse-macos` | 147 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the pinned Eclipse macOS preset, and internal/app/settings_pages.go exposes every preset in the native Keymap settings page. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and selection |
| `eclipse-windows` | 148 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the pinned Eclipse Windows preset, and internal/app/settings_pages.go exposes every preset in the native Keymap settings page. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and selection |
| `intellij-macos` | 370 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the complete pinned 370-binding IntelliJ macOS preset plus the Windows, Eclipse and VS Code presets. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and native keymap selection |
| `intellij-windows` | 370 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the complete pinned IntelliJ Windows preset, and internal/app/settings_pages.go exposes every preset in the native Keymap settings page. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and selection |
| `vscode-macos` | 372 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the pinned VS Code macOS preset, and internal/app/settings_pages.go exposes every preset in the native Keymap settings page. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and selection |
| `vscode-windows` | 41 | integrated - internal/app/keymap_presets_generated.go ships the pinned VS Code Windows preset including its inherited bindings, and internal/app/settings_pages.go exposes every preset in the native Keymap settings page. TestKeymapPresetRegistryMatchesPinnedOpenIDEASet, TestKeymapPresetSurfacesShowAllSixChoices and TestKeymapPresetChangeRefreshesTheSharedNativeMenu cover the generated bindings and selection |

Counts are the bindings written literally in a preset file. A preset may spread
another (`vscode-windows` translates and extends `vscode-macos`), so its effective
map is larger; the union above and the keymap-only ids below are complete.
GoIDEA's key handling is its own: a preset id does not imply that GoIDEA ships the
same bindings until a check records it.

Generate Maven Sources runs one named POM once or every non-aggregated project sequentially, refreshes Maven only after success and stops cleanly, covered by maven_generate_sources_test.go. maven_test.go and build_tools_parity_test.go cover the UI, and real headless SmokeMaven loaded 3 modules, 3 profiles, 20 plugins and a dependency tree; real Add/Exclude mutation acceptance remains |
| `notifications` | Notifications | right | `src/workbench/activate.ts` | integrated - internal/app/notifications.go balloons the newest three entries with OpenIDEA's exact timeout rules: info and warnings last 10 seconds, default errors need five minutes of active-window time when logged, explicitly non-sticky errors use 10 seconds, and unlogged sticky balloons stay until dismissed. Hover and application deactivation pause the remaining clock instead of restarting it. The right-stripe Notifications window marks logged entries read, hides their balloons and keeps unlogged balloons visible, and a project switch drops prior project notifications except application updates. TestNotificationTimeoutMatchesOpenIDEA, TestNotificationClockPausesOutsideTheActiveWindow, TestNotificationHoverResumesTheRemainingTime, TestUnloggedBalloonStaysVisibleBesideTheNotificationsWindow, TestProjectSwitchKeepsOnlyApplicationUpdateNotifications and TestNotificationsWindowRendersOpenIDEAHeader cover these rules. Keyed replacement, actions, Clear All/Hide, unread badges, verbatim plugin text, history persistence, per-group Balloon/Sticky/No popup/Log, Don't Show Again and sound are covered by notifications_test.go |
| `problems` | Problems | bottom | `src/features/code-insight/index.ts` | integrated - internal/app/problems.go problemsPanel; lsp.problems.errors, lsp.problems.rows, lsp.problems.visible; internal/app/problems_test.go |
| `profiler` | Profiler | bottom | `src/features/profiler/index.ts` | integrated - internal/app/dockregistry.go registers the bottom Profiler tool window and profiler_view.go renders snapshot tabs, flame graph, call tree, methods, search and empty/error states; profiler.window passes after the real Node.js CPU profile |
| `project` | Project | left | `src/features/project-tree/index.ts` | integrated - internal/workspace tree tests; base.tabs |
| `pullRequests` | Pull Requests | left | `src/features/github/index.ts` | partial - internal/app/github_view.go registers Pull Requests in the dock's left primary slot, with token setup, filters, details, files, review and normal diffs, threads, metadata/review forms, timeline comments and the create form. TestGitHubPullRequestsDockMovesThroughTheLeftPrimaryArea covers opening, hiding, moving to the right, returning to the left and surviving hide/show. TestGitHubToolWindowRendersNativeStatesOffscreen covers major states. Visual comparison with OpenIDEA remains |
| `remote.ports` | Ports | bottom | `src/features/remote-dev/index.ts` | partial - The pinned OpenIDEA fork now supplies remote_forwarded_ports, remote_forward_port and remote_stop_forwarding. internal/app/remote_ports.go implements the native Ports tool window, per-host/folder persistence, configured container-port separation, restore after reconnect, bounded tunnels and process-group cleanup. TestRemotePortsPersistManualForwardsButNotGeneratedContainerPorts, TestRemotePortsRestoreManualForwardsOnReconnect, TestRemotePortStopClosesListenerAndRemovesPersistence, TestRemotePortsRestoreFailureIsVisible and TestRemoteContainerTunnelKeepsTheReplyAfterClientHalfClose cover the flow; a live external Docker/WSL acceptance remains |

TestDeploymentActionsAndNativeSurfacesAreRegistered and TestDeploymentAutoUploadWaitsForAuthRetryAndThenContinues cover registration and the native flow |

TestRunWindowUnifiedTabsSessionRailAndEmptyState, TestRunConsoleControlsChangeNativePaneState, TestRunConsoleStdinAndEofUseNativeRunRPCs, TestRunTestResultsUseTheSessionSplitAndSelectedOutput, TestServiceSessionShownInRunUsesTheSameNativeSessionSurface, TestRunTabsUseOpenIDEARemoteTargetLabels and TestRunFinishedTabTooltipClosingLastTabAndFailureState cover the unified tabs, vertical rail, service-session reuse, console controls and stdin, selected-test output, remote labels, failure icon, finished tooltip, empty state and hiding the window with its final tab |

| `editor.general.editorTabs` | Editor Tabs |  | `src/features/settings/pages/editor/general.tsx` | integrated - internal/app/settings_pages.go settingsEditorTabsBody: Tab placement Top/Bottom/Left/Right/None, Show tabs in (one row scrolling the strip sideways, squeezing tabs down to their fixed parts, or wrapping into multiple rows) over Preferences.Tabs.MultipleRows/SqueezeTabs, Show file icon, Show pinned tabs in a separate row, Show file extension, Show directory for non-unique file names, Mark modified (*), Show full path on mouse hover, Close button position Right/Left/None, Sort tabs alphabetically, Open new tabs at the end, Opening Policy's Enable preview tab, Tab limit 1-100 and When the current tab is closed, activate left/right/mru over Preferences.Tabs feeding the workbench tab strip (internal/app/workbench.go tabStrip/tabButtons: a ScrollHorizontal row with a tracked scroll state, a squeeze row whose labels ellipsize to the tab's fixed parts, and a wrapped row; left or right turns it into a 140-260 wide vertical column of full-width 24px scrolling tabs beside the editor with the divider on the editor side and the pinned group in its own column, internal/app/workbench.go verticalTabs; Show tabs in is disabled and its label dimmed for left, right and none; the active tab is OpenIDEA's tinted box with a 1px selected border; activating a tab scrolls it into view; a scrolling strip whose tabs run past its width shows OpenIDEA's chevron at its end, whose Show Hidden Tabs menu lists every tab in the strip with the active one highlighted over tabOverflow/tabMenuRows, internal/app/workbench.go), insertion/sort/limit and the close activation policy (internal/app/tabs.go nextActiveAfterClose, internal/app/app.go removeTab), staged through settingsDraft and proven by settings_test.go TestSettingsEditorTabsStageAndApply, TestSettingsTabOverflowAndFileIconStageAndApply and tabs_test.go TestTabLabelFollowsTheStripSettings, TestInsertTabAfterTheActiveOneOrAtTheEnd, TestSortTabsByTitlePinsFirstAndKeepsTheActiveTab, TestTabLimitCandidatesKeepPinnedActiveAndUnsaved, TestNextActiveAfterCloseFollowsThePolicy, TestTabActivationDefaultsToTheLeft, TestPreviewTabPromotesAndIsNotPinned, TestTabMenuRowsNumberTheStripAndMarkTheActiveTab, TestTabOrderSplicesTheLiftedTabIntoItsDrop, TestReorderTabsMovesTheTabAndFollowsTheActiveOne, TestTabDropIndexTakesTheRowAndTheSlotMidpoint; a tab dragged along its row lifts out of the strip after a 4px threshold, leaves its hover-tinted slot where it would drop while the other tabs make room, and a translucent ghost of it follows the pointer until letting go reorders the strip, over tabDrag, tabDragPress, tabOrder, reorderTabs, tabDropIndex and tabGhost in internal/app/workbench.go, following OpenIDEA's previewOrder splice and dropIndex row/midpoint rule; the tab context menu adds Close with Cmd+W, Keep Tab Open for a preview tab, Open In (Project View, the file manager and a terminal at the file's folder), Local History (Show History and Put Label), Copy Path/Reference (absolute, file name and, inside the project, the path from the content root) and Rename File for a file tab, then Configure Editor Tabs, over tabMenu and tabOpenInMenu in internal/app/workbench.go with tabPathFlavours in internal/app/editor_menu.go, proven by editor_menu_test.go TestTabPathFlavoursCopyInsideAndOutsideTheProject; the file icon is one generic glyph where OpenIDEA draws per-file-type icons, the chevron reads the strip's overflow from the last frame's scroll extent so it can appear a frame after the tabs grow, horizontal and vertical strips share row-aware dragging; dropping on an editor edge creates or moves the other group, proven by TestReorderTabsInRangeKeepsAVerticalStripDragInsideItsRow, the tab menu's Git group is ported for Commit File…, Add, Annotate, Show Diff, Show History, Rollback… and Branches… (its branch and revision compares, push/pull/fetch, merge/rebase, new branch/tag, reset, stash and GitHub entries are not) and Compare With…, Compare with Clipboard and Local History are available over internal/app/diff.go while window.detachTab still moves the selected tab into the other editor split group rather than a complete native project window. **File › Open Project in New Window** is available at the project level through internal/app/workspace_host.go, while Window › Editor Tabs remains the two-group shared-document subset proven by editor_groups_test.go. The closing-policy radios now differ safely: unsaved tabs never close automatically, while unchanged-mode prefers previews and never-visited tabs before LRU and lru-mode uses activation order, proven by TestTabLimitClosingPoliciesChooseDifferentSafeCandidates |

### Keys

Every fixed key reachable from the engine's `Settings` type (`crates/ide-core`),
flattened to dotted camelCase paths, plus keys an option on a page edits.
Collection keys (`Vec`, maps) are one item: their entries are data, not fixed
settings.

| Key | Edited on | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `actionsOnSave` |  | missing |
| `actionsOnSave.cleanup` |  | integrated - internal/app/save_actions_settings.go reads actionsOnSave.cleanup and runs the backend code-action cleanup step after optimize imports while Save Files is enabled; TestActionsOnSaveRunOptimizeImportsThenCleanup covers the ordered runtime path |
| `actionsOnSave.optimizeImports` |  | integrated - internal/app/save_actions_settings.go reads actionsOnSave.optimizeImports and invokes source.organizeImports before cleanup; TestActionsOnSaveRunOptimizeImportsThenCleanup covers the real save sequence |
| `agentSecurity` |  | missing |
| `agentSecurity.allowedCommands` |  | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go loads `agentSecurity.allowedCommands` from the complete OpenIDEA settings root, and internal/app/settings_runtime.go maps it into the existing native runtime consumer. TestNativeRuntimeCatalogConsumesFullSettingsRoot, TestNativeRuntimeCatalogMapsOnlyConsumedKeys and the consumer-specific settings tests cover load, project-overlay and Apply behavior. |

TestEncodedProjectTextOpensThroughDocOpenInsteadOfHex proves encoded text reaches doc_open instead of the hex viewer, TestNewEmptyFileDefersEncodingAndBOMToBackendCreateFile proves empty creation uses fs_create_file, and TestIntegrationFileEncodingsDriveDocumentsAndNewFiles proves GBK project decoding and both UTF-8 BOM policies with exact settings_set keys and file bytes |

| `mcpServer.agentArchiveLimit` | agents.mcpServer | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go exposes Agents › IDE MCP Server with OpenIDEA's 0..100000 bound and 999 default in global scope. internal/app/run_agent_sessions.go maintains real native agent sessions, computes the archive from finished runs newest-first, and removes entries beyond the limit oldest-first while never archiving a live run. settings_native_catalog.go trims on load and settings_backend_catalog.go trims after Apply. TestAgentRunLimitsArchiveOldestFinishedTabsAndNeverLiveOnes, TestAgentRunSettingPageLoadsAndStagesGlobalLimits, TestAgentRunSettingPageRendersLimitsAndGlobalScope, TestAgentRunLimitsApplyTrimsArchive and TestMCPAgentRunClosedNotifiesOnlyForUserClosedTabs cover the behavior and the mcp_agent_run_closed notification |
| `mcpServer.agentTabLimit` | agents.mcpServer | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go exposes Agents › IDE MCP Server with OpenIDEA's 0..1000 bound and 1 default in global scope. internal/app/run_agent_actions.go runs distinct agent names concurrently through the real run_start/run_stop lifecycle, while run_agent_sessions.go and run_agent_view.go retain finished same-name tabs, show only the newest configured number outside the archive and notify mcp_agent_run_closed when the user closes one. TestRunAgentStartBuildsUpstreamConfigAndRejectsLiveName, TestAgentRunLimitsArchiveOldestFinishedTabsAndNeverLiveOnes, TestAgentRunSettingPageLoadsAndStagesGlobalLimits, TestAgentRunSettingPageRendersLimitsAndGlobalScope, TestAgentRunLimitsApplyTrimsArchive and TestMCPAgentRunClosedNotifiesOnlyForUserClosedTabs cover the behavior |
| `mcpServer.enabled` | agents.mcpServer | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go exposes Enable MCP Server with OpenIDEA's false default in global scope, and settings_set starts or stops the real HTTP server immediately. TestMCPServerSettingsLoadStageAndSaveGlobally and TestNativeRuntimeSettingsLoadBeforePageNavigation cover global loading and staging; settings_start_http_server_and_stdio_bridge_and_stop_both calls mcp_server_status before and after enable/disable and proves Discovery and Stdio clients reach the host |
| `mcpServer.runWithoutConfirmation` | agents.mcpServer | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go exposes brave mode with OpenIDEA's false default in global scope, and the pinned backend reads mcpServer.runWithoutConfirmation through global_settings in ide-host/src/mcp/tool.rs. TestMCPServerSettingsLoadStageAndSaveGlobally and TestNativeRuntimeSettingsLoadBeforePageNavigation cover global loading and staging; settings_start_http_server_and_stdio_bridge_and_stop_both proves the normal confirm path and real mcp_respond Allow/Deny round trip |

| `vcs.commit.subjectLimit` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go uses the configured subject limit for validation and the editor margin. TestVCSCommitMessageInspections covers the boundary |
| `vcs.commit.wrapAtMargin` |  | integrated - internal/app/commit.go wraps typed commit bodies at the configured margin. TestVCSCommitMessageInspections covers the wrapper output |
| `vcs.confirmation.confirmDropCommits` | vcs.confirmation | integrated - internal/app/vcs_rebase_dialog.go requires confirmation before interactive rebase drops commits when enabled. TestVCSInteractiveRebaseConfirmsDrops covers the guarded operation |
| `vcs.confirmation.onCreate` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_file_confirmation.go resolves created paths through vcs_status and supports Ask, Add silently and Do not add, including folder contents, Unity metadata and Don't ask again. TestVCSFileOperationMatchingAddsFoldersAndUnityMetadata and TestVCSFileOperationNotificationHonorsNeverWithoutContactingBackend cover matching and the no-contact mode |
| `vcs.confirmation.onDelete` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_file_confirmation.go resolves deleted working-tree rows and supports Ask, Remove silently and Do not remove. TestVCSFileOperationMatchingUsesDeletedWorkingTreeRows covers deletion matching and the native action updates the index |
| `vcs.confirmation.onPatchCreated` | vcs.confirmation | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go and vcs_more_dialog.go implement Ask, No and Reveal for newly created patches. TestVCSPatchAndShelfModes and TestVCSPatchAskOpensNativeConfirmation cover the choices and confirmation sheet |
| `vcs.confirmation.showUpdateOptions` | vcs.confirmation | integrated - internal/app/vcs_operations.go opens the native Pull strategy chooser only while Show options before Update is enabled. TestVCSProtectedBranchesAndPushDialogDecision covers runtime settings and VCS operation tests cover the Pull path |
| `vcs.git.autoUpdateOnPushRejected` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/vcs_operations.go shows the native Push Rejected Merge/Rebase sheet, persists the remembered update method and retries up to ten times through vcs_remote. TestRejectedPushOpensMergeChoiceAndRetriesInOrder, TestVCSRejectedPushStopsAfterTenPushes and TestPushRejectedRememberPersistsRuntimeChoices cover the full choice, retry and persistence paths |
| `vcs.git.combineStashAndShelf` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/vcs_toolwindow.go combines stashes and shelves into one native content tab when enabled. TestVCSProjectTreeDecorationsAndCombinedStashTabs covers the combined tab |
| `vcs.git.executable` |  | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go loads `vcs.git.executable` from the complete OpenIDEA settings root, and internal/app/settings_runtime.go maps it into the existing native runtime consumer. TestNativeRuntimeCatalogConsumesFullSettingsRoot, TestNativeRuntimeCatalogMapsOnlyConsumedKeys and the consumer-specific settings tests cover load, project-overlay and Apply behavior. |
| `vcs.git.incomingCheck` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go applies Auto, Always and Never to background incoming-commit checks. TestVCSHistoryLimitOnlyCapsPathHistory and the VCS runtime suite cover the setting's runtime behavior |
| `vcs.git.inlineBlame` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/inline_blame.go renders the current line's author, relative date and summary through the MyGo inlay layer after a 300 ms idle delay, merges with other inlays and suppresses virtual, read-only, large, untracked and ignored files. TestInlineBlameFormattingAndMergedInlay covers formatting and merge behavior |
| `vcs.git.largeFileMb` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go rejects oversized included files before commit using the configured MiB threshold. TestVCSCommitChecksWarnBeforeCommit covers the large-file inspection |
| `vcs.git.protectedBranches` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go parses semicolon-separated branch patterns and disables Force Push on protected branches. TestVCSProtectedBranchesAndPushDialogDecision covers main, wildcard, question-mark and detached-head behavior |
| `vcs.git.pushDialogOnlyProtected` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go limits the Push sheet to branches matching the protected-branch patterns when enabled. TestVCSProtectedBranchesAndPushDialogDecision covers the branch matching and runtime decision |
| `vcs.git.showBranchInProjectView` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/project_view.go shows the branch and incoming/outgoing counts in the Project view only when enabled. TestVCSProjectTreeDecorationsAndCombinedStashTabs covers project-tree runtime state |
| `vcs.git.showPushDialog` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go decides whether Commit and Push opens the native Push sheet. TestVCSProtectedBranchesAndPushDialogDecision covers enabled, disabled and protected-branch combinations |
| `vcs.git.signOff` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/commit.go initializes the native Sign-off option from the Git setting and sends it in CommitOptions. TestVCSCommitDefaultsDraftAndNonModalMode covers the runtime default |
| `vcs.git.updateMethod` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go supplies the configured Merge or Rebase method to Pull and rejected-Push recovery, while vcs_operations.go saves a chosen method when the branch has no outgoing merge commits. TestVCSProtectedBranchesAndPushDialogDecision and TestPushRejectedRememberPersistsRuntimeChoices cover runtime selection and persistence |
| `vcs.git.warnBadFileNames` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go rejects names that are invalid on Windows when the setting is enabled. TestVCSCommitChecksWarnBeforeCommit covers the inspection |
| `vcs.git.warnCrlf` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go detects CRLF text about to be committed when the setting is enabled. TestVCSCommitChecksWarnBeforeCommit covers the inspection |
| `vcs.git.warnDetachedHead` | vcs.git | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go warns for detached HEAD or rebase state when enabled. TestVCSCommitChecksWarnBeforeCommit covers the before-commit problem path |
| `vcs.git.warnLargeFiles` |  | integrated - internal/app/vcs_settings_runtime.go enables the large-file before-commit inspection when configured. TestVCSCommitChecksWarnBeforeCommit covers the warning and Commit Anyway path |
| `vcs.historyLimit` |  | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go loads `vcs.historyLimit` from the complete OpenIDEA settings root, and internal/app/settings_runtime.go maps it into the existing native runtime consumer. TestNativeRuntimeCatalogConsumesFullSettingsRoot, TestNativeRuntimeCatalogMapsOnlyConsumedKeys and the consumer-specific settings tests cover load, project-overlay and Apply behavior. |
| `vcs.issueNavigation` |  | integrated - internal/app/settings_issue_navigation.go and Preferences.VCS.IssueNavigation carry OpenIDEA's Issue ID regular expressions and Issue Link templates; internal/app/settings_window.go stages, applies, persists and resets them, while internal/app/issue_links.go applies earliest-match-wins capture expansion and HTTP(S) links in Git Log, commit details and blame; issue_links_test.go and settings_test.go TestSettingsIssueNavigationStagesAppliesAndEdits and TestIssueNavigationEditorCommitsWithEnterAndCancelsWithEscape |
| `vcs.limitHistory` |  | integrated - internal/app/settings_native_catalog.go loads `vcs.limitHistory` from the complete OpenIDEA settings root, and internal/app/settings_runtime.go maps it into the existing native runtime consumer. TestNativeRuntimeCatalogConsumesFullSettingsRoot, TestNativeRuntimeCatalogMapsOnlyConsumedKeys and the consumer-specific settings tests cover load, project-overlay and Apply behavior. |

## IPC commands

All 668 commands the desktop shell registers. `stdio` is whether the pinned
`openidea-server` serves the command; `GoIDEA` is whether GoIDEA calls it.
Local-only commands and the per-command argument lists are in
[OPENIDEA_IPC.md](OPENIDEA_IPC.md).

| Command | Module | stdio | GoIDEA | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `agent_accept_changes` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agents.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_archive` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agent_workspace.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_base_text` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agent_diff.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_cancel` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agents.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_changes` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agents.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_commands` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agent_composer.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_create` | agent | yes | yes | integrated - internal/app/agents.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_custom_import` | agent | no |  | integrated - internal/app/agent_registry_actions.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_custom_remove` | agent | no |  | integrated - internal/app/agent_registry_actions.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |
| `agent_custom_save` | agent | no |  | integrated - internal/app/agent_registry_actions.go invokes the OpenIDEA RPC through the native Go adapter |

| `sdk_project_libraries` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/libraries.go calls sdk_project_libraries with request, cancel_request and onGroups streaming; internal/app/libraries_smoke.go and libraries.* run it against the pinned openidea-server with SDK groups and an expanded JDK source archive |
| `sdk_python_create_venv` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go sends tool, base, location, request and onOutput; internal/app/language_sdks.go exposes cancellable venv, uv, Poetry and Conda creation with bounded output and session cleanup. language_sdks_test.go covers the channel handshake, toolchains.python creates a real venv through the shipped backend, and extendedToolchains.pythonEnvironments creates real isolated uv, Poetry and Conda environments |
| `sdk_python_effective` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go wraps optional near and decodes the effective interpreter; internal/app/python_packages.go resolves it before listing or mutating the Dependencies page for the current project and rejects stale replies. TestPythonPackagesLoadTheEffectiveInterpreter and TestPythonPackagesIgnoreAProjectSwitch cover resolution and project ownership, while toolchains.python proves a selected created virtualenv resolves against the real backend |
| `sdk_python_interpreter` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go validates an executable or virtualenv directory; internal/app/language_sdks.go probes hand-entered choices and reports invalid paths, covered by language_sdks_test.go. toolchains.python validates the host python3 and created virtualenv against the real backend |
| `sdk_python_packages` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go sends python plus request and decodes package name/version rows; internal/app/python_packages.go lists the effective interpreter's packages on Settings › Python › Dependencies with search, Refresh, deterministic Freeze, Copy and Save. TestPythonPackagesLoadTheEffectiveInterpreter, TestPythonPackagesFilterAndFreezeDeterministically and TestPythonPackagesIgnoreAProjectSwitch cover the native flow, and extendedToolchains.pythonPackages lists a real uv environment after install, upgrade and uninstall |
| `sdk_python_pip` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go sends install, upgrade or uninstall requirements with a cancellable request and onOutput channel; internal/app/python_packages.go provides owned, bounded package jobs, cancellation, error display and automatic package-list refresh. TestPythonPackagesLoadTheEffectiveInterpreter, TestPythonPackageJobUsesOwnedOutputChannelAndRefreshes, TestPythonPackageJobCanBeCanceled and TestPythonPackagesIgnoreAProjectSwitch cover the native flow, and extendedToolchains.pythonPackages performs real uv install, upgrade and uninstall with standards-compliant local wheels before listing and freezing the resulting environment |
| `sdk_python_project_interpreters` | sdk | yes | yes | integrated - internal/openidea/toolchains.go decodes recent-project interpreter rows; internal/app/language_sdks.go loads them into All Interpreters, labels their project owners, deduplicates shared paths, supports hiding other projects and rejects stale replies. TestPythonInterpreterPageLoadsMixedScopesAndSavesEachFieldInItsScope and TestBuildPythonInterpreterRowsKeepsCurrentVisibleAndDropsHiddenOthers cover the native merge and filtering, while toolchains.python calls the command against the real backend |

549 commands are served over stdio and 447 are called by GoIDEA today. A command
that is registered only in the desktop shell needs an `ops` module before stdio
can serve it; a GoIDEA check counts only when the UI path works end to end.

## Events and channels

### Backend events

| Enum | Variant | Wire type | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| IdeEvent | `DbSourcesChanged` | `dbSourcesChanged` | integrated - internal/openidea/parity_events.go decodes dbSourcesChanged into DBSourcesChangedEvent; internal/app/event_runtime.go refreshes only an already-open native Database pane through db_sources/db_connected. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeDBAndTrustEventsStayOnTheirProject cover the payload and stale-project behavior |
| IdeEvent | `McpRequest` | `mcpRequest` | integrated - internal/app/run.go decodes mcpRequest and posts it to internal/app/mcp_requests.go; TestMCPConfirmQueueAnswersAllowDenyAndExpiry, TestMCPOpenFileUsesZeroBasedCoordinatesAndReportsFailures and TestMCPAgentRunAnswersExactlyOnceAfterStart cover confirm, openFile and agentRun, and settings_start_http_server_and_stdio_bridge_and_stop_both emits the event from a real MCP client |
| IdeEvent | `McpRequestClosed` | `mcpRequestClosed` | integrated - internal/app/run.go decodes mcpRequestClosed and internal/app/mcp_requests.go removes the matching queued or in-flight response without re-showing it. TestMCPClosedRequestDoesNotReturnAfterFailedAnswer covers the closed-event lifecycle; settings_start_http_server_and_stdio_bridge_and_stop_both verifies expiry before mcp_ui_ready on the real backend |
| IdeEvent | `WindowGroupsChanged` | `windowGroupsChanged` | blocked - Pinned OpenIDEA marks windowGroupsChanged desktop-local and ide-host drops it before stdio. GoIDEA owns native window groups directly in internal/app/parity_window_groups.go; TestWindowGroupActionsOnNativeHost covers the native host flow. There is no server event to route |
| IdeEvent | `WindowFullscreenChanged` | `windowFullscreenChanged` | blocked - Pinned OpenIDEA marks windowFullscreenChanged desktop-local and ide-host drops it before stdio. GoIDEA owns native fullscreen state through internal/app/zen.go and its window host; no server event can be claimed over stdio |
| IdeEvent | `ProjectTrustChanged` | `projectTrustChanged` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go accepts projectTrustChanged only for the open root, updates the existing trust gate, reconnects documents and reloads run configurations, libraries and Project Structure on grant, and clears stale run/library state on revoke. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeDBAndTrustEventsStayOnTheirProject cover decode and runtime behavior |
| IdeEvent | `FsChanged` | `fsChanged` | integrated - internal/app/run.go routes fsChanged to internal/app/remote_fs_events.go, which refreshes loaded project folders, reloads clean editors, preserves unsaved text and refreshes VCS/derived indexes. TestDecodeIDEEvent, TestChangedParentFoldersDeduplicatesCreatedModifiedAndRemoved, TestAcceptFsChangedReloadsCleanRemoteEditor and TestAcceptFsChangedKeepsUnsavedRemoteEditor cover the runtime path |
| IdeEvent | `ExcludedDirsChanged` | `excludedDirsChanged` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go refreshes loaded native Project View directories for excludedDirsChanged, ignoring paths outside the open project and coalescing duplicate directories. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeExcludedDirsRefreshOnlyLoadedTree cover decode and the native tree path |
| IdeEvent | `DocPatched` | `docPatched` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `DocDirty` | `docDirty` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `WorkspaceEditApplied` | `workspaceEditApplied` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `DocLanguage` | `docLanguage` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `Diagnostics` | `diagnostics` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `Task` | `task` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go maps backend task events to the native background-task balloon with prefixed ids, numeric cancel_task requests, done settling and root checks. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeBackendTaskCancelUsesNumericTaskIDAndDropsStaleRoot cover the payload, cancellation and stale-window path |
| IdeEvent | `IndexState` | `indexState` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go maps indexState to OpenIDEA's uncancellable native Indexing task with file and symbol counts and refreshes readiness. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeIndexTaskIsUncancellableAndUpdatesReadiness cover the state and native task |
| IdeEvent | `VcsChanged` | `vcsChanged` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go accepts vcsChanged for the open root, refreshes native VCS, branch and visible log panes, and invalidates gutter, blame and Code Vision caches. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeVCSChangedRefreshesAndDropsStaleDecorations cover the runtime route and stale-root guard |
| IdeEvent | `LanguageServer` | `languageServer` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `LanguageServerWarning` | `languageServerWarning` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `LanguageServerRemoved` | `languageServerRemoved` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `LspRefresh` | `lspRefresh` | partial - internal/app/event_runtime.go invalidates native semantic tokens for semanticTokens and native inlay hints for inlayHints; TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeLSPRefreshInvalidatesOnlySupportedConsumers cover both. Remaining gap: GoIDEA has no native LSP code-lens consumer, so codeLens refreshes are recorded as unsupported rather than claimed |
| IdeEvent | `TerminalExited` | `terminalExited` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `RunExited` | `runExited` | missing |
| IdeEvent | `Debug` | `debug` | integrated - internal/app/run.go routes debug envelopes to internal/app/debug.go with session ownership and stale-generation checks. TestDebugSessionsKeepIndependentStateAndOutput, TestDebugPluginLateSessionEventDoesNotSettleNewerLaunch and TestDebugHeapProgressRoutesByOwnerAndChecksOwnershipFirst cover independent sessions, stale events and owner-tagged progress |
| IdeEvent | `Notify` | `notify` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go maps notify info, warning and error levels into the existing native notification center and balloons. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeNotifyAndProjectRootsUseNativeState cover decode and native notification state |
| IdeEvent | `SettingsChanged` | `settingsChanged` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go coalesces settingsChanged for 30 ms, reloads native runtime and file-type settings, and refreshes build, MCP, SSH and encoding consumers by key with root checks. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeSettingsChangedCoalescesAndDropsStaleTimers cover decode, coalescing and stale-project drops |
| IdeEvent | `UpdateStatusChanged` | `updateStatusChanged` | blocked - Pinned OpenIDEA marks updateStatusChanged desktop-local and ide-host drops it before stdio. GoIDEA owns equivalent direct update state in internal/app/update.go and its native update dialog; TestCheckForUpdatesAnnouncesAsAStickyNotification covers that direct flow. The server event cannot be claimed over stdio |
| IdeEvent | `UpdateAvailable` | `updateAvailable` | blocked - Pinned OpenIDEA marks updateAvailable desktop-local and ide-host drops it before stdio. GoIDEA owns equivalent update availability and installation state directly in internal/app/update.go; TestCheckForUpdatesAnnouncesAsAStickyNotification covers the native notification path. The server event cannot be claimed over stdio |
| IdeEvent | `ProjectRootsChanged` | `projectRootsChanged` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go applies projectRootsChanged only to the open root and refreshes the native Project View, Project Structure SDKs, build model and run configurations. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeNotifyAndProjectRootsUseNativeState cover decode and the runtime route |
| IdeEvent | `Askpass` | `askpass` | partial - internal/app/event_runtime.go queues and displays the native askpass prompt and TestRuntimeAskpassQueuesAndDropsWithoutClaimingAnAnswerRPC covers de-duplication and the visible limitation. Remaining gap: ssh_askpass_answer exists only in OpenIDEA's desktop shell and is absent from the stdio dispatcher, so GoIDEA cannot send an answer |
| IdeEvent | `AskpassDone` | `askpassDone` | integrated - internal/app/event_runtime.go removes the matching native askpass prompt on askpassDone. TestDecodeIDEEvent and TestRuntimeAskpassQueuesAndDropsWithoutClaimingAnAnswerRPC cover the payload and queue removal |
| IdeEvent | `NotebookKernel` | `notebookKernel` | missing |
| McpUiRequest | `Confirm` | `confirm` | missing |
| McpUiRequest | `OpenFile` | `openFile` | missing |
| McpUiRequest | `AgentRun` | `agentRun` | missing |

The desktop shell forwards these as one Tauri event (`ide://event`). Over stdio
they are `event` notifications; high-volume streams use `channel` notifications
instead (RPC_PROTOCOL.md).

## Backend feature modules (crates)

| Crate | Role | Linked into `ide-host` (stdio reachable) | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ide-agent` | Coding agents over the Agent Client Protocol: tasks, sessions, worktrees and their timelines | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-analysis` | Project-wide code analysis over syntax trees: duplicate detection (Locate Duplicates) and structural search & replace | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-android` | Android devices and logcat for the Logcat tool window, driven through the adb CLI | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-build` | Build tool models for the Maven and Gradle tool windows: POM parsing, Gradle task lists, background sync | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-cli` | The `openidea` command: arguments, the request it sends a running app over a local socket, and installing it on PATH | no | missing |
| `ide-clipboard` | The OS clipboard's file list (Explorer <-> project view): Windows CF_HDROP or macOS NSPasteboard file URLs, no window or message loop | no | missing |
| `ide-core` | Shared vocabulary of OpenIDEA: ids, text coordinates, events, errors, settings | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-coverage` | Run with Coverage: coverage-tool launch plans (cargo-llvm-cov, vitest / jest / c8, coverage.py, go test, JaCoCo) and report parsers (lcov, Go cover profiles, JaCoCo XML) | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-dap` | Debug adapter protocol client: sessions, breakpoints, threads, frames, variables, evaluation | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-db` | Database tools: data sources, connections, introspection, query execution and table editing | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-docker` | Docker integration for the Services tool window, driven through the docker CLI | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-extension` | Plugin manifest, extension points, contribution registry and capability model (no Wasm runtime) | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-fonts` | Font families installed on this machine, for the font pickers: CoreText, DirectWrite or fontconfig | no | missing |
| `ide-github` | GitHub pull requests through the gh CLI | no | missing |
| `ide-heap` | HotSpot .hprof parser and heap-snapshot analysis for the debugger's JVM Memory view | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-host` | The IDE backend without a UI toolkit: projects per window, terminals, processes, and the JSON-RPC server for remote development | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-http` | HTTP Client: .http request files, environments, execution and response history | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-index` | Background project index: files and symbols, persisted in redb, fuzzy queried with nucleo | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-jdwp` | JDWP codec and a transparent debugger link that counts every object ID it receives, so it can release them exactly | no | missing |
| `ide-jsonrpc` | Content-Length framed transport and JSON-RPC 2.0 peer shared by LSP and DAP clients | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-jvm-attach` | HotSpot attach-socket client and class-histogram parser for the debugger's JVM Memory view | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-jvm-inspect` | Shared types and the JvmInspector trait for inspecting live JVM instances while a debug session is suspended | no | missing |
| `ide-language` | Language registry (languages/*.toml), detection, and tree-sitter powered outline/folding/symbol tags | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-lsp` | Language server manager: lifecycle, document sync, feature requests mapped to IDE types | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-mcp` | The IDE's MCP server: Streamable HTTP on 127.0.0.1 for AI agents outside the IDE, its discovery file and the stdio bridge | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-notebook` | Lossless native notebook documents and owned Jupyter kernels | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-plugin` | WebAssembly runtime for plugins (wasmtime component model + WASI preview 2) | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-profiler` | Run with Profiler: sampling-profiler launch plans (Node.js --cpu-prof, py-spy, async-profiler, Linux perf) and a common call-tree model from .cpuprofile, collapsed stacks and perf script output | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-remote` | Remote work over SSH: SFTP deployment (mappings, upload, download, sync) and run targets (SSH, Docker, Compose) | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-run` | Run configurations: detection, persistence, process lifecycle and output streaming | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-scaffold` | New Project generators: the generator catalog (`generators/*.toml`), project templates, official framework CLIs and initializr-style project services | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-sdk` | SDKs / toolchains: interpreter discovery (Python, Node.js, Rust, Go, TypeScript), virtualenvs, pip, and applying the selected toolchain to language servers, run configurations and terminals | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-search` | Find / Replace in Files built on the ripgrep engine crates | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-spell` | Spell checking (Proofreading › Typo): word extraction from comments, strings and declared names, Hunspell en_US / en_GB dictionary, programming terms, user dictionaries | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-ssh` | SSH connections: configurations, host keys, authentication, remote commands, SFTP and FTP files, port forwarding and askpass | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-syntax` | Tree-sitter structure service: node/expression lookup, syntax errors, scopes & locals, identifier occurrences, structural ranges (LSP fallback) | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-terminal` | Integrated terminal PTY sessions | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-text` | Rope-backed documents, change sets and UTF-16/byte coordinate conversion | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-tools` | Installer for language servers and debug adapters: registry, detection, download/extract, managed tools dir | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-vcs` | Git integration: gitoxide for fast reads, git CLI for writes | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-vfs` | File system access: directory listing, text loading/saving with encoding detection, watching, project walking | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-web` | Web browsers: the built-in static web server that serves project files on 127.0.0.1 (IDEA's built-in server) and detection / launching of installed browsers | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |
| `ide-workspace` | Project model: open documents, settings layering, recent projects, session state, local history | yes | engine-only - linked into ide-host |

`engine-only` means the crate is linked into the stdio host, so its logic can be
reached; it does not mean GoIDEA has a UI for it. A crate that is not linked
needs an OpenIDEA change before any GoIDEA work can call it.

## Plugin extension points

Plugins are sandboxed WebAssembly components (`wit/`, interface version 1.5.0).
The base world imports the host API and exports three interfaces; optional worlds
add exports. GoIDEA does not load plugins yet, so every entry starts as `missing`.

## What this means for GoIDEA

The matrix is the port backlog, ordered by product surface rather than by what
Mayoi happens to contain. An action, tool window or page is not ported because a
similar Mayoi one exists; it is ported when the OpenIDEA interaction behind it
works in GoIDEA and a gate check proves it. Backend work goes upstream through the
OpenIDEA fork and a submodule bump, never by patching the submodule in place.
